style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">$ sed -n <span class="hljs-string">'50,200p'</span> /home/runner/work/nn.nn/nn.nn/lang/a9nn/AtomSpace.lua
_atomID = _atomID + 1
   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> setmetatable({
      <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>       = _atomID,
      <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>     = atomType,
      name     = name    or <span class="hljs-string">""</span>,
      outgoing = outgoing or {},   -- list of atom IDs (<span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> Links)
      tv       = tv or TruthValue.new(1, 1),
      av       = av or AttentionValue.new(0, 0),
      incoming = {},               -- <span class="hljs-built_in">set</span> of <span class="hljs-built_in">link</span> IDs that reference this atom
   }, Atom)
end

<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> Atom:isNode() <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> <span class="hljs-comment">#self.outgoing == 0 end</span>
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> Atom:isLink() <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> <span class="hljs-comment">#self.outgoing &gt; 0  end</span>

<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> Atom:__tostring()
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> self:isNode() <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> string.format(<span class="hljs-string">"(%s \"%s\" %s)"</span>, self.type, self.name, tostring(self.tv))
   <span class="hljs-keyword">else</span>
      <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> string.format(<span class="hljs-string">"(%s %s %s)"</span>, self.type,
         table.concat(self.outgoing, <span class="hljs-string">","</span>), tostring(self.tv))
   end
end

-- ─── AtomSpace ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

<span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> AtomSpace, _ = Class.class(<span class="hljs-string">'nn.AtomSpace'</span>)

<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> AtomSpace:__init()
   self.atoms     = {}    -- <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span> → Atom
   self.nodeIndex = {}    -- <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>+name → <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>
   self.linkIndex = {}    -- <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>+outgoing_key → <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>
   self.typeIndex  = {}   -- <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span> → {<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>, ...}
end

--- Add or retrieve a Node.
-- @param atomType  string, e.g. <span class="hljs-string">"ConceptNode"</span>
-- @param name      string identifier
-- @param tv        TruthValue (optional)
-- @<span class="hljs-built_in">return</span>          Atom
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> AtomSpace:addNode(atomType, name, tv)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> key = atomType .. <span class="hljs-string">"|"</span> .. name
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> self.nodeIndex[key] <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> existing = self.atoms[self.nodeIndex[key]]
      <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> tv <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span> existing.tv = tv end
      <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> existing
   end
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> atom = Atom.new(atomType, name, {}, tv)
   self.atoms[atom.id]    = atom
   self.nodeIndex[key]    = atom.id
   self.typeIndex[atomType] = self.typeIndex[atomType] or {}
   table.insert(self.typeIndex[atomType], atom.id)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> atom
end

--- Add or retrieve a Link.
-- @param atomType  string, e.g. <span class="hljs-string">"InheritanceLink"</span>
-- @param outgoing  list of Atom objects (or IDs)
-- @param tv        TruthValue (optional)
-- @<span class="hljs-built_in">return</span>          Atom
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> AtomSpace:addLink(atomType, outgoing, tv)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> ids = {}
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> _, a <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> ipairs(outgoing) <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      ids[#ids+1] = <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>(a) == <span class="hljs-string">"table"</span> and a.id or a
   end
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> key = atomType .. <span class="hljs-string">"|"</span> .. table.concat(ids, <span class="hljs-string">","</span>)
   <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> self.linkIndex[key] <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
      <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> existing = self.atoms[self.linkIndex[key]]
      <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> tv <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span> existing.tv = tv end
      <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> existing
   end
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> atom = Atom.new(atomType, <span class="hljs-string">""</span>, ids, tv)
   self.atoms[atom.id]   = atom
   self.linkIndex[key]   = atom.id
   self.typeIndex[atomType] = self.typeIndex[atomType] or {}
   table.insert(self.typeIndex[atomType], atom.id)
   -- Update incoming sets
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> _, <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> ipairs(ids) <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> self.atoms[<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>] <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
         self.atoms[<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>].incoming[atom.id] = <span class="hljs-literal">true</span>
      end
   end
   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> atom
end

--- Retrieve atom by <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>.
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> AtomSpace:getAtom(<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> self.atoms[<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>]
end

--- Retrieve node by <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>+name.
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> AtomSpace:getNode(atomType, name)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> key = atomType .. <span class="hljs-string">"|"</span> .. name
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>  = self.nodeIndex[key]
   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span> and self.atoms[<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>] or nil
end

--- Return all atoms of a given <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>.
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> AtomSpace:getAtomsOfType(atomType)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> out   = {}
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> ids   = self.typeIndex[atomType] or {}
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> _, <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> ipairs(ids) <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      out[#out+1] = self.atoms[<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>]
   end
   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> out
end

--- Incoming <span class="hljs-built_in">set</span> of an atom (links that reference it).
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> AtomSpace:getIncoming(atom)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> out = {}
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> pairs(atom.incoming) <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      out[#out+1] = self.atoms[<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>]
   end
   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> out
end

--- Simple pattern matching: <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> links of given <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span> whose outgoing <span class="hljs-built_in">set</span>
--- contains all of the supplied atoms (<span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> any position).
<span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> AtomSpace:getLinks(linkType, atoms)
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> mustHave = {}
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> _, a <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> ipairs(atoms or {}) <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      mustHave[<span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>(a)==<span class="hljs-string">"table"</span> and a.id or a] = <span class="hljs-literal">true</span>
   end
   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> results = {}
   <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> _, atom <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> pairs(self.atoms) <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
      <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> atom.type == linkType and atom:isLink() <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span>
         <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> match = <span class="hljs-literal">true</span>
         <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> pairs(mustHave) <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
            <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> found = <span class="hljs-literal">false</span>
            <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> _, oid <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> ipairs(atom.outgoing) <span class="hljs-keyword">do</span>
               <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> oid == <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span> <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span> found = <span class="hljs-literal">true</span>; <span class="hljs-built_in">break</span> end
            end
            <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> not found <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span> match = <span class="hljs-literal">false</span>; <span class="hljs-built_in">break</span> end
         end
         <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> match <span class="hljs-keyword">then</span> results[#results+1] = atom end
      end
   end
   <span class="hljs-built_in">return</span> results
end

--- Remove an atom (and clean up indices).

   <span class="hljs-built_in">local</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">id</span>   = <span class="hljs-built_in">type</span>(atomOrId) == <span class="hljs-string">"table"</span> and atomOrId.<span class="hljs-built_in">id</span> or atomOrId

@module plingua_compat
{
    /* Standard evolution rule wrapper */
    @macro evolution_rule(lhs, rhs, membrane)
    {
        [lhs]<span class="hljs-string">'membrane -&gt; [rhs]'</span>membrane
    }

    /* Symport rule: move objects across membrane boundary */
    @macro symport_in(objects, membrane)
    {
        (objects, <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span>) -&gt; [objects]<span class="hljs-string">'membrane
    }

    @macro symport_out(objects, membrane)
    {
        [objects]'</span>membrane -&gt; (objects, out)
    }

    /* Antiport rule: exchange objects across membrane boundary */
    @macro antiport(objects_in, objects_out, membrane)
    {
        [objects_out]<span class="hljs-string">'membrane (objects_in, in) -&gt;
        [objects_in]'</span>membrane (objects_out, out)
    }

    /* Dissolution rule: dissolve membrane, objects go to parent */
    @macro dissolve(membrane, objects)
    {
        [objects]<span class="hljs-string">'membrane -&gt; objects     /* membrane dissolved */
    }

    /* Division rule: one membrane becomes two */
    @macro divide(membrane, left_objects, right_objects)
    {
        [membrane] -&gt; [left_objects]'</span>new_left [right_objects]<span class="hljs-string">'new_right
    }

    /* Probabilistic rule: fire lhs -&gt; rhs with probability p (0-100).
     * Maps onto native probabilistic P-System rules where supported;
     * otherwise uses the random-guard convention (see bayesian.pli). */
    @macro prob_rule(p, lhs, rhs, membrane)
    {
        [lhs]'</span>membrane -&gt; [rhs]<span class="hljs-string">'membrane where random(0, 100) &lt; p
    }

    /* Priority annotation */
    @macro high_priority(rule)
    {
        @priority(1) rule
    }

    @macro low_priority(rule)
    {
        @priority(99) rule
    }
}
&lt;shellId: 3 completed with exit code 0&gt;

    @rules
    {
        /* v = 0.9*20 + 10 = 28 */
        [velocity{1, 20}, grad{1, 10}, beta{90}]'</span><span class="hljs-built_in">test</span> -&gt;
            [velocity{1, 28}]<span class="hljs-string">'test

        assert_equal("momentum_velocity", 28, velocity{1})

        /* w = 100 - 0.1*28 ~ 97 */
        [weight{1, 100}, velocity{1, 28}, learning_rate{10}]'</span><span class="hljs-built_in">test</span> -&gt;
            [weight{1, 97}]<span class="hljs-string">'test

        assert_equal("momentum_weight_update", 97, weight{1})
    }
}

@test adam_test
{
    @description "Adam: bias-corrected first/second moment update"

    @setup
    {
        [test_adam
            m{1, 0}
            v{1, 0}
            grad{1, 100}
            timestep{1}
            beta1{90}   /* 0.9 */
            beta2{99}   /* 0.999 approximated as 0.99 */
        ]'</span>main
    }

    @rules
    {
        /* m = 0.9*0 + 0.1*100 = 10 */
        [m{1, 0}, grad{1, 100}, beta1{90}]<span class="hljs-string">'test -&gt;
            [m{1, 10}]'</span><span class="hljs-built_in">test</span>

        assert_equal(<span class="hljs-string">"adam_first_moment"</span>, 10, m{1})

        /* v = 0.99*0 + 0.01*100^2/100 = 100 */
        [v{1, 0}, grad{1, 100}, beta2{99}]<span class="hljs-string">'test -&gt;
            [v{1, 100}]'</span><span class="hljs-built_in">test</span>

        assert_equal(<span class="hljs-string">"adam_second_moment"</span>, 100, v{1})

        /* Bias correction at t=1: m_hat = m / (1 - 0.9) = 100 */
        [m{1, 10}, timestep{1}, beta1{90}]<span class="hljs-string">'test -&gt;
            [m_hat{1, 100}]'</span><span class="hljs-built_in">test</span>

        assert_equal(<span class="hljs-string">"adam_bias_correction"</span>, 100, m_hat{1})
    }
}

/* ============================================================================
 * NORMALIZATION TESTS
 * ============================================================================ */

@<span class="hljs-built_in">test</span> batch_norm_test
{
    @description <span class="hljs-string">"Batch norm: zero-mean/unit-variance in train mode"</span>

    @setup
    {
        [test_bn
            /* Batch of 2 values <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> feature 1: 40 and 60; mean = 50 */
            batch_value{1, 1, 40}
            batch_value{2, 1, 60}
            mode{train}
        ]<span class="hljs-string">'main
    }

    @rules
    {
        /* mean = (40 + 60) / 2 = 50 */
        [batch_value{1, 1, 40}, batch_value{2, 1, 60}]'</span><span class="hljs-built_in">test</span> -&gt;
            [batch_mean{1, 50}]<span class="hljs-string">'test

        assert_equal("bn_batch_mean", 50, batch_mean{1})

        /* variance = ((40-50)^2 + (60-50)^2)/2 = 100 */
        [batch_mean{1, 50}]'</span><span class="hljs-built_in">test</span> -&gt;
            [batch_var{1, 100}]<span class="hljs-string">'test

        assert_equal("bn_batch_variance", 100, batch_var{1})
    }
}

9.7c-.2.2-.4.3-.6.3Z"></path></svg></span><span class="NewToolHeader-module__text__mpVp6"><span class="NewToolHeader-module__title__voLMB">Read GNN message passing conventions</span></span></button></div><div><div class="Tool-module__toolContentMissionControl__fqP1_"><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">$ sed -n <span class="hljs-string">'1,120p'</span> /home/runner/work/nn.nn/nn.nn/lang/pli/gnn.pli
/*
 * Graph Neural Network Modules <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> PLingua
 * Membranes as graph nodes: each graph node is a membrane [node{i} ...]
 * holding feature objects feat{d, v}; edges are edge{i, j, w} objects
 * <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> the enclosing graph membrane, so the membrane structure itself is
 * the graph and message passing maps onto inter-membrane communication.
 *
 * Modules:
 *   - message_passing: send/aggregate neighbor features (<span class="hljs-built_in">sum</span>/mean/max)
 *   - gcn_layer:       degree-normalized graph convolution + backward
 *   - graph_attention_layer: GAT-style per-edge attention coefficients
 *   - graph_readout:   global <span class="hljs-built_in">sum</span>/mean/max pooling to a graph embedding
 *
 * Degree normalization: node degrees deg{i} are precomputed <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> @initial
 * (or accumulated from edge objects); aggregated features are divided
 * by deg to give the symmetric-normalization approximation used by GCN.
 * Self-loops: edge{i, i, 100} objects (weight 1.0) are added <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span>
 * @initial so every node includes its own features <span class="hljs-keyword">in</span> the aggregate.
 *
 * Numerical convention: values scaled by 100, consistent with nn.pli.
 */

@model&lt;membrane_computing&gt;

/* ============================================================================
 * MESSAGE PASSING
 * For each edge (i -&gt; j), copy the <span class="hljs-built_in">source</span> node<span class="hljs-string">'s features into the
 * target node as msg{j, d, v} objects, then reduce incoming messages
 * per node with the pairwise-reduction idiom from attention.pli.
 * ============================================================================ */

@module message_passing(num_nodes, feat_dim)
{
    @initial
    {
        [graph
            for (i = 1; i &lt;= num_nodes; i++)
            {
                [node{i}]'</span>graph
                /* Self-loop so a node sees its own features */
                edge{i, i, 100}
            }
        ]<span class="hljs-string">'main
    }

    @rules send_messages
    {
        /* Node i exposes its features to the graph membrane */
        [feat{d, v}]'</span>node{i} -&gt;
            [feat{d, v}]<span class="hljs-string">'node{i} (node_feat{i, d, v}, out)

        /* Each edge routes a weighted copy to the target node */
        [node_feat{i, d, v}, edge{i, j, w}]'</span>graph -&gt;
            [node_feat{i, d, v}, edge{i, j, w},
             msg{j, d, round(v*w/100)}]<span class="hljs-string">'graph

        /* Deliver messages into the target node membrane */
        [msg{j, d, v}]'</span>graph -&gt;
            [msg{d, v}]<span class="hljs-string">'node{j}
    }

    @rules aggregate_sum
    {
        /* Pairwise sum of incoming messages per dimension */
        [msg{d, v1}, msg{d, v2}]'</span>node{j} -&gt;
            [msg{d, v1+v2}]<span class="hljs-string">'node{j}

        [msg{d, v}, agg_done{d}]'</span>node{j} -&gt;
            [agg{d, v}]<span class="hljs-string">'node{j}
    }

    @rules aggregate_mean
    {
        /* Mean variant: divide the summed messages by the node degree */
        [agg{d, v}, deg{n}, use_mean{}]'</span>node{j} -&gt;
            [agg{d, round(v/n)}, deg{n}]<span class="hljs-string">'node{j}
    }

    @rules aggregate_max
    {
        /* Max variant: pairwise max instead of sum */
        [msg{d, v1}, msg{d, v2}, use_max{}]'</span>node{j} -&gt;
            [msg{d, max(v1, v2)}, use_max{}]<span class="hljs-string">'node{j}
    }
}

/* ============================================================================
 * GCN LAYER
 * Graph convolution: aggregate neighbor features weighted by
 * degree-normalized edge weights, then apply a shared linear
 * projection and ReLU. Backward rules propagate gradients back
 * along the (reversed) edges, in the simplified style of nn.pli.
 * ============================================================================ */

@module gcn_layer(num_nodes, in_dim, out_dim)
{
    @initial
    {
        [gcn
            /* Shared projection weights across all nodes */
            for (d1 = 1; d1 &lt;= in_dim; d1++)
            {
                for (d2 = 1; d2 &lt;= out_dim; d2++)
                {
                    weight{d1, d2, random(-50, 50)}
                }
            }
            for (d2 = 1; d2 &lt;= out_dim; d2++)
            {
                bias{d2, 0}
            }

            for (i = 1; i &lt;= num_nodes; i++)
            {
                [node{i}
                    deg{1}      /* self-loop counts once; incremented per edge */
                ]'</span>gcn
                edge{i, i, 100}
            }
        ]<span class="hljs-string">'main
&lt;shellId: 5 completed with exit code 0&gt;
</span></code></pre></div></figure></div></div></div></div><div class="Tool-module__toolContainer__PQCpK"><div class="ToolGroupRenderer-module__toolGroupHeader__zA5xT"><div MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf tmp-p-3" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><h2>Phase 1 — <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/atomspace.pli</code>: P-Systems Hypergraph AtomSpace</h2>
<p>Port <code node="[object Object]">a9nn/AtomSpace.lua</code> semantics into native membrane computing.</p>
<ul class="contains-task-list">
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Write <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/atomspace.pli</code> — atom nodes/links as membranes, TruthValue (strength×count) + revision, AttentionValue (STI/LTI), ECAN-style attention spreading (antiport wages/income), Hebbian link formation, pattern matcher, attentional focus</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Append 8 atomspace tests to <code node="[object Object]">test_extensions.pli</code></li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox"> Append 2 atomspace demos to <code node="[object Object]">demo.pli</code></li>

## Phase <span class="hljs-number">1</span> — `lang/pli/atomspace.pli`<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> P-Systems Hypergraph AtomSpace

Port `a9nn/AtomSpace.lua` semantics into native membrane computing.

- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> Write `lang/pli/atomspace.pli` — atom nodes/links as membranes<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> TruthValue (strength×count) + revision<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> AttentionValue (STI/LTI)<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> ECAN-style attention spreading (antiport wages/income)<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> Hebbian link formation<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> pattern matcher<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> attentional focus
- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> Append <span class="hljs-number">8</span> atomspace tests to `test_extensions.pli`
- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> Append <span class="hljs-number">2</span> atomspace demos to `demo.pli`
- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> Register `atomspace` in `integration.pli` module_registry
- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> Update `lang/pli/README.md` with AtomSpace section
- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> `bash lang/pli/validate.sh` passes for all files
- <span class="hljs-punctuation">[</span> <span class="hljs-punctuation">]</span> Secret scan + CodeQL + commit/push

Numerical convention<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> values scaled ×<span class="hljs-number">100</span> (integers)<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> matching nn.pli/gnn.pli. No change to the shared XOR fixture (no float forward/loss semantics added)<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> so cross-port consistency is preserved.

@module test_framework
{
    /* Test result accumulator */
    @initial
    {
        [test_suite
            total_tests{0}
            passed_tests{0}
            failed_tests{0}
        ]<span class="hljs-string">'main
    }
    
    /* Assert equal: check if two values are equal */
    @rules assert_equal(name, expected, actual)
    {
        [test_name{name}, expected{e}, actual{a}]'</span><span class="hljs-built_in">test</span> -&gt;
        {
            /* Pass <span class="hljs-keyword">if</span> equal (within epsilon <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> floats) */
            [result{pass}]<span class="hljs-string">'test where abs(e - a) &lt; 0.01
            [total_tests{t}]'</span>suite -&gt; [total_tests{t+1}, passed_tests{p+1}]<span class="hljs-string">'suite
            
            /* Fail if not equal */
            [result{fail}]'</span><span class="hljs-built_in">test</span> <span class="hljs-built_in">where</span> abs(e - a) &gt;= 0.01
            [total_tests{t}]<span class="hljs-string">'suite -&gt; [total_tests{t+1}, failed_tests{f+1}]'</span>suite
            []<span class="hljs-string">'test (error{name, e, a}, out)
        }
    }
    
    /* Report test results */
    @rules report
    {
        [total_tests{t}, passed_tests{p}, failed_tests{f}]'</span>suite -&gt;
        {
            []<span class="hljs-string">'suite (test_summary{
                total: t,
                passed: p,
                failed: f,
                success_rate: (p*100)/t
            }, out)
        }
    }
}

@demo greedy_decoding
{
    @description <span class="hljs-string">"Autoregressive greedy decoding of a 4-token sequence"</span>

    @membrane_structure
    {
        [main
            [gen
                step{1}
                tgt_token{1, 1}    /* position 1 holds the BOS token */
                vocab_size{5}
                eos_token{4}
            ]<span class="hljs-string">'main
        ]
    }

    @execution
    {
        /* Step 1 logits (last position 1): argmax over ids 1..5 is 2 */
        [step{1}]'</span>gen -&gt; [logit{1, 1, 10}, logit{1, 2, 90},
                          logit{1, 3, 40}, logit{1, 4, 20},
                          logit{1, 5, 60}, step{1}]<span class="hljs-string">'gen

        greedy_decode(5, 4, 4)

        []'</span>main (display{
            <span class="hljs-string">"Greedy Decoding (max_len = 4)"</span>,
            <span class="hljs-string">"Step 1 argmax"</span>: 2, <span class="hljs-string">"Step 2 argmax"</span>: 5,
            <span class="hljs-string">"Step 3 argmax"</span>: 3, <span class="hljs-string">"Step 4 argmax"</span>: 4,
            <span class="hljs-string">"Generated sequence"</span>: [1, 2, 5, 3],
            <span class="hljs-string">"Token 4 is EOS: generation halted"</span>
        }, out)
    }
}

/* ============================================================================
 * MAIN DEMO RUNNER
 * ============================================================================ */

@main
{
    @description <span class="hljs-string">"Run all demonstrations"</span>
    
    []<span class="hljs-string">'main (display{
        "========================================",
        "Neural Network in PLingua - Demo Suite",
        "========================================",
        "",
        "This demo showcases the P-Systems implementation",
        "of neural networks using membrane computing.",
        ""
    }, out)
    
    /* Menu */
    []'</span>main (display{
        <span class="hljs-string">"Available Demos:"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"1. Basic Network Creation"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"2. XOR Problem (Classic Test)"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"3. Activation Functions Comparison"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"4. Loss Functions"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"5. Multi-Layer Network"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"6. Training Visualization"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"7. Binary Classification"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"8. Optimizer Comparison (SGD vs Adam)"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"9. LeNet-5 Convolutional Network"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"10. LSTM Sequence Prediction"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"11. Attention Weights"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"12. Spiking Neural P System XOR"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"13. GNN Node Classification (Toy Graph)"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"14. Evolving XOR Weights (Neuroevolution)"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"15. Bayesian Uncertainty (Noisy Regression)"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"16. Greedy Decoding (4-Token Sequence)"</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">""</span>,
        <span class="hljs-string">"Running all demos..."</span>
    }, out)

    /* Run all demos */
    basic_network
    xor_problem
    activation_functions
    loss_functions
    multilayer_network
    training_visualization
    binary_classification
    optimizer_comparison
    lenet5_demo
    lstm_sequence_demo
    attention_demo
    snp_xor_demo
    gnn_node_classification
    neuroevolution_xor
    bayesian_uncertainty
    greedy_decoding
    
    /* Conclusion */

        "",
        "========================================",
        "All Demos Complete!",
        "========================================",
        "",
        "Key Concepts Demonstrated:",
        "- P-Systems model neural computations",

5:## Overview
9:## Features
11:### Core Functionality
19:### P-Systems Implementation
26:### Modular Components
32:- **Graph Neural Networks**: Message passing, GCN, graph attention (GAT), graph readout — membranes as graph nodes
33:- **Neuroevolution**: Genome membranes, mutation, crossover, division-based reproduction, tournament selection, NEAT-style topology evolution
34:- **Bayesian Layers**: Weight distributions, reparameterization trick, KL regularization, Monte-Carlo uncertainty estimates
35:- **Transformer Decoder**: Causal masking, masked self-attention, cross-attention, decoder stacks, positional encodings, greedy decoding

240:### Neuroevolution (neuroevolution.pli)
255:### Bayesian Layers (bayesian.pli)
270:### Transformer Decoder (transformer_decoder.pli)
677:- [x] Probabilistic P-Systems <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> Bayesian layers — `bayesian.pli`
678:- [x] Full transformer decoder with causal masking — `transformer_decoder.pli`
726:| `test_extensions.pli` | 30 tests covering all extension, v2.1 and v2.2 modules |
728:## Extensions (v2.2)
734:| `bayesian.pli` | Probabilistic P-Systems <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> Bayesian layers: Gaussian sampler (central-limit), Bayes-by-Backprop linear layer, KL divergence regularizer, Monte-Carlo predictive mean/variance |
735:| `transformer_decoder.pli` | Full transformer decoder with causal masking: masked self-attention, cross-attention, masked multi-head attention, decoder blocks/stacks, sinusoidal positional encoding, full encoder-decoder transformer, greedy decoding |
=== around v2.2 modules doc ===
@module graph_readout(num_nodes, feat_dim)
/* Global <span class="hljs-built_in">sum</span>/mean/max pooling into a graph-level embedding */
```

<span class="hljs-comment">### Neuroevolution (neuroevolution.pli)</span>

<span class="hljs-comment">#### Divide and Mutate</span>
```plingua
@module divide_and_mutate(fitness_threshold)
/* Fit genome membranes divide (elite + mutated offspring); */
/* unfit genomes dissolve - native P-Systems division/dissolution */
```

<span class="hljs-comment">#### Neuroevolution Loop</span>
```plingua
@module neuroevolution_loop(pop_size, generations, mutation_rate)
/* Orchestrates evaluate -&gt; <span class="hljs-keyword">select</span> -&gt; divide/mutate phases */
```

<span class="hljs-comment">### Bayesian Layers (bayesian.pli)</span>

<span class="hljs-comment">#### Bayesian Linear Layer</span>
```plingua
@module bayes_linear_layer(input_size, output_size)
/* Weight distributions w_mu / w_rho; reparameterization trick */
/* w = mu + softplus(rho) * eps, eps ~ N(0, 1) */
```

<span class="hljs-comment">#### Monte-Carlo Prediction</span>
```plingua
@module mc_predict(num_samples)
/* Stochastic forward passes -&gt; pred_mean / pred_var (uncertainty) */
```

<span class="hljs-comment">### Transformer Decoder (transformer_decoder.pli)</span>

<span class="hljs-comment">#### Masked Self-Attention</span>
```plingua
@module masked_self_attention(seq_len, d_k)
/* Causal `<span class="hljs-built_in">where</span> j &lt;= i` guard: no attention to future positions */
```

<span class="hljs-comment">#### Decoder Block and Full Transformer</span>
```plingua
@module transformer_decoder_block(tgt_len, src_len, d_model, num_heads, d_ff)
@module full_transformer(vocab_size, src_len, tgt_len, d_model, num_heads, d_ff, num_layers)
/* Masked self-attention -&gt; cross-attention -&gt; FFN, each add&amp;norm */
```

<span class="hljs-comment">#### Greedy Decoding</span>
```plingua
@module greedy_decode(vocab_size, max_len, eos_token)
/* Autoregressive generation via argmax over last-position logits */
```

<span class="hljs-comment">### Inference</span>

<span class="hljs-comment">#### Predict</span>
```plingua
@module predict(network, input)
/* Use trained network <span class="hljs-keyword">for</span> predictions */
/* Sets evaluation mode (no gradients) */
```

<span class="hljs-comment">## Examples</span>

<span class="hljs-comment">### Example 1: Simple Linear Regression</span>

Learn the <span class="hljs-keyword">function</span> y = 2x₁ + 3x₂

```plingua
/* Network: 2 inputs -&gt; 1 output (no hidden layer) */
[network linear_layer(2, 1)]<span class="hljs-string">'main

/* Training data */
training_data{
    sample{[10, 10], 50},   /* 2×10 + 3×10 = 50 */
    sample{[20, 10], 70},   /* 2×20 + 3×10 = 70 */
    sample{[10, 20], 80}    /* 2×10 + 3×20 = 80 */
}

/* Train for 100 epochs with learning rate 0.1 */
train_network(network, training_data, 100, 10)

/* Predict for new input [25, 15] */
&lt;shellId: 14 completed with exit code 0&gt;

<span class="hljs-comment">### Test Coverage (core: test_nn.pli)</span>

- ✅ Batch normalization statistics (train mode)
- ✅ Dropout masks and eval-mode identity

- ✅ LSTM cell-state gate evolution
- ✅ Gradient clipping by value
- ✅ LeakyReLU, ELU, LogSoftMax activations
- ✅ BCE, CrossEntropy, SmoothL1 criteria
- ✅ CAddTable, Identity, Reshape containers
- ✅ LookupTable embedding lookup
- ✅ Scaled dot-product attention scores
- ✅ SN P neuron firing and sub-threshold behavior
- ✅ Exporter save/load weight round-trip
- ✅ GCN degree-normalized aggregation and graph readout pooling
- ✅ Membrane-division reproduction and tournament selection
- ✅ Bayesian reparameterized weights and Monte-Carlo mean
- ✅ Causal mask and decoder-block residual connection

data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><h2>Phase 1 — <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/atomspace.pli</code>: P-Systems Hypergraph AtomSpace</h2>
<p>Port <code node="[object Object]">a9nn/AtomSpace.lua</code> semantics into native membrane computing.</p>
<ul class="contains-task-list">
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> Write <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/atomspace.pli</code> — atom nodes/links as membranes (structural hypergraph nesting), TruthValue (strength×count) + count-based revision, AttentionValue (STI/LTI), ECAN attention spreading (conserved-currency antiport wage→income), Hebbian link formation, pattern matcher, derived attentional focus</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> Append 8 atomspace tests to <code node="[object Object]">test_extensions.pli</code> (30 → 38 total)</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> Append 2 atomspace demos to <code node="[object Object]">demo.pli</code> (ECAN spreading, Hebbian learning; 16 → 18 total)</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> Register <code node="[object Object]">atomspace</code> in <code node="[object Object]">integration.pli</code> module_registry (v2.3)</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> Update <code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/README.md</code> — feature bullet, AtomSpace API section, roadmap checkbox, v2.3 extensions table, test/demo counts, coverage list</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> <code node="[object Object]">bash lang/pli/validate.sh</code> passes for all 24 files</li>
<li class="task-list-item" node="[object Object]"><input disabled="" type="checkbox" checked=""> Secret scan clean; CodeQL N/A (no analyzable languages)</li>
</ul>
<p>Numerical convention: values scaled ×100 (integers), matching nn.pli/gnn.pli. No change to the shared XOR fixture (no float forward/loss semantics added), so cross-port consistency is preserved.</p></div><div data-component="Stack" class="prc-Stack-Stack-UQ9k6" data-gap="condensed" data-direction="vertical" data-align="stretch" data-wrap="nowrap" data-justify="start" data-padding="normal"><span class="fgColor-muted prc-Text-Text-9mHv3" data-component="Text" data-size="small" data-weight="medium">Output</span><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf border rounded bgColor-default overflow-hidden" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><figure class="ToolCodeBlock-module__container__EQXTk"><div class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeContainer__YJrZ9"><pre class="ToolCodeBlock-module__code__X8Whj"><code class="ToolCodeBlock-module__codeWrap__HyFh6">Committed and pushed<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> feat(pli)<span class="hljs-punctuation">:</span> add AtomSpace hypergraph KB with ECAN<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> Hebbian learning<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> matcher<span class="hljs-punctuation">,</span> focus (v2<span class="hljs-number">.3</span>)

<p>1 row(s) updated.</p></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><div class="CopilotMessageBubble-module__container__oW7JC"><div class="markdown-body MarkdownRenderer-module__container__sJ8jf CopilotMessageBubble-module__text__ADukm" data-copilot-markdown="true" style="--MarkdownRenderer_streaming-fade-duration: 750ms;"><p>Phase 1 complete ✅ — <code node="[object Object]">atomspace.pli</code> is implemented, tested, documented, and pushed.</p>
<h2>What was built</h2>
<p><strong><code node="[object Object]">lang/pli/atomspace.pli</code></strong> (new, ~330 lines) — an OpenCog-style hypergraph AtomSpace ported from <code node="[object Object]">a9nn/AtomSpace.lua</code> into native membrane computing:</p>
<ul>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>Atoms as membranes</strong> — <code node="[object Object]">atom_node</code> (typed/named leaf) and <code node="[object Object]">atom_link</code> (a membrane that physically <em>contains</em> its endpoint atoms, so hypergraph nesting is structural, not a data structure)</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>Truth values</strong> — <code node="[object Object]">tv{s,c}</code> with count-based revision (<code node="[object Object]">s'=(s₁c₁+s₂c₂)/(c₁+c₂)</code>), matching <code node="[object Object]">TruthValue:confidence()</code> semantics</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>ECAN attention spreading</strong> — STI as a <em>conserved currency</em>: focused atoms pay a wage that flows as income to link-neighbours via antiport exchange. One maximally-parallel step diffuses STI across the whole focus — the O(1)-transition capability imperative ports can't reproduce</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>Hebbian learning</strong> — co-focused atoms emit simultaneous pulses; a pulse pair wires a symmetric <code node="[object Object]">HebbianLink</code> ("fire together, wire together")</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>Pattern matcher</strong> (<code node="[object Object]">query_type</code> / <code node="[object Object]">query_link</code>) and a <strong>derived attentional focus</strong> (<code node="[object Object]">in_focus{}</code> flagged by threshold, not stored)</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Supporting changes:</strong></p>
<ul>
<li node="[object Object]"><code node="[object Object]">test_extensions.pli</code>: +8 tests → <strong>38 total</strong> (creation, nesting, TV revision, STI conservation, Hebbian wiring, type-match, focus mark/departure)</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><code node="[object Object]">demo.pli</code>: +2 demos → <strong>18 total</strong> (ECAN spreading, Hebbian learning)</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><code node="[object Object]">integration.pli</code>: registry entry <code node="[object Object]">module{atomspace, "1.0.0", [nn, integration]}</code> (v2.3)</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><code node="[object Object]">README.md</code>: feature bullet, full AtomSpace API section, roadmap checkbox, v2.3 table, updated counts/coverage</li>
</ul>
<p><strong>Validation:</strong> all 24 <code node="[object Object]">.pli</code> files pass <code node="[object Object]">validate.sh</code>; secret scan clean; CodeQL N/A (no analyzable languages). No change to the shared XOR fixture, so cross-port consistency holds.</p>
<hr>
<h3>Next up — your call 🙂</h3>
<ol>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong><code node="[object Object]">a9nn.pli</code></strong> — NNECCO cognitive agent on top of atomspace.pli (EchoReservoir membrane, Personality, EpisodicMemory, 12-step EchoBeats as rule phases, LLaMA pool as load-balanced membranes)</li>
<li node="[object Object]"><strong>P-Limbo design doc</strong> — scope the Inferno/Dis distributed-grid variant as an exploratory document (it'd be a separate <code node="[object Object]">lang/limbo</code> port, not PLingua)</li>
</ol>
<p>My recommendation is <strong>a9nn.pli</strong> next, since it directly reuses the AtomSpace foundation we just laid. Want me to proceed?</p></div></div><div class="SessionFooter-module__footer__uEbNi SessionFooter-module__metadataBar__VBpWD"><div class="SessionFooter-module__actions__unnyo"><div
